| G182 | The user writes a snippet at the resolution question | One editor for a kind a manager tracks, two for an unowned path, and the entry is resolved only when each carries something | U | `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_add_snippet_choice_captures_the_body_the_user_wrote`; `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_a_package_backed_kind_opens_one_editor`; `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_an_unowned_path_records_both_bodies` |
| G183 | The user is about to write an unowned path's installed-version snippet | Its own editor screen names both machines and says it must change nothing — the obligation pc-switcher cannot check | U | `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_the_authoring_warning_is_on_the_editors_own_screen` |
| G190 | A hand-deb, sideload or bundle-flatpak entry carrying an installed-version snippet | Malformed too: the manager answers that question, so nothing would ever run it | U | `test_package_state:TestSnippetRegistry::test_every_malformed_entry_in_one_registry_is_named_at_once` |

### G.11 Removing what the source dropped (articles: PKG-FR-MANUAL-REMOVE, PKG-NG-MANUAL-REMOVE-REACH)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| G160 | Nomad has a sideload Atlas lacks | Offered for removal; `snap remove` keeps snapd's own pre-removal snapshot | U V | `test_manual_snap_sync:TestWhatTheTargetAlreadyHolds::test_a_sideload_only_the_target_holds_is_offered_for_removal`; `test_package_sync:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_each_job_removes_its_own_kind_of_finding_from_the_target` |
| G161 | Nomad has a STORE snap Atlas lacks | Nothing here: it is `snap_sync`'s to remove, and deleting it would take software off the update path it is on | U | `test_manual_snap_sync:TestWhatTheTargetAlreadyHolds::test_a_store_snap_only_the_target_holds_produces_nothing` |
| G168 | Nomad has a hand-installed `.deb` Atlas lacks | Offered for removal: Nomad's own apt says no repository supplies it | U V | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_a_hand_installed_deb_the_source_dropped_is_offered_for_removal`; `test_package_sync:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_each_job_removes_its_own_kind_of_finding_from_the_target` |
| G169 | Nomad has a package Atlas lacks that its own repositories supply | Never offered here — that is `apt_sync`'s decision, with its own bookkeeping behind it | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_a_package_a_repository_supplies_is_never_offered_for_removal` |
| G170 | An approved `.deb` removal | `apt-get remove`, never `purge`: what apt leaves under `/etc` can be deleted by hand, and a purge cannot be undone | U V | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_the_removal_runs_apt_get_remove_and_never_purge`; `test_package_sync:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_each_job_removes_its_own_kind_of_finding_from_the_target` |
| G171 | Nomad recorded the item machine-specific, and Atlas has since dropped it | No removal: the mark keeps Nomad's own copy, and both machines' marks are read before anything is offered | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_a_mark_on_the_target_keeps_its_own_copy_off_the_removal_list` |
| G176 | Nomad has an unowned path Atlas lacks | Offered for removal: an unowned path is this job's finding whichever machine it is on | U V | `test_manual_installs_sync:TestRemovingAPathTheSourceDropped::test_a_path_only_the_target_holds_is_offered_for_removal`; `test_package_sync:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_each_job_removes_its_own_kind_of_finding_from_the_target` |
| G177 | An approved path removal | The path is deleted and nothing else, and the screen says a launcher or a symlink the same recipe dropped elsewhere stays | U V | `test_manual_installs_sync:TestRemovingAPathTheSourceDropped::test_the_removal_deletes_the_path_and_the_screen_says_what_it_leaves`; `test_package_sync:TestRemovingWhatTheSourceDropped::test_each_job_removes_its_own_kind_of_finding_from_the_target` |
| G184 | Nomad has a bundle-installed flatpak app Atlas lacks | Offered for removal with `flatpak uninstall` naming the full reference in its own scope, privileged only where that scope is `system` | U | `test_manual_flatpak_sync:TestRemovingARefTheSourceDropped::test_a_bundle_ref_only_the_target_holds_is_uninstalled_in_its_own_scope` |


## H. Consent, the review, the answers, and the memory of a decision

### H.1 Nothing is modified before its approval, per job (articles: PKG-FR-REVIEW-FIRST, PKG-FR-CONSENT-BEFORE-CHANGE)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H1 | A package job runs with items to decide. | The job plans, reviews, accepts the outcome, runs its after-review seam, then applies — in that order, and never applies before the review returns. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_call_order_is_plan_review_accept_review_apply` |
| H2 | `apt_sync` plans against Atlas and Nomad. | No mutating command reaches Nomad during planning: no `apt-get install`, no `sudo install`, no `sudo rm`, no `sudo apt-get`, no `sudo cp`. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestPlanApplySplit::test_plan_issues_no_mutating_command` |
| H3 | `snap_sync` plans against a diverged pair. | No `snap install`, `snap refresh`, `snap switch` or `snap remove` is issued while planning. | U | `test_snap_sync:TestPlanReadOnly::test_plan_issues_no_mutating_snap_command` |
| H4 | `flatpak_sync` plans against a diverged pair. | No `flatpak install`, `flatpak uninstall`, `remote-add` or `remote-delete` is issued while planning. | U | `test_flatpak_sync:TestPlanReadOnly::test_plan_issues_no_mutating_flatpak_command` |
| H5 | `manual_installs_sync` plans (scan + registry read). | No mutating command and no `send_file` reaches Nomad while planning. | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestPlanIsReadOnly::test_plan_issues_no_mutating_command_and_transfers_nothing` |
| H6 | Any job plans while a decision file exists on either machine. | No decision-file write (`mv --force`) is issued on either machine during planning. | U | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_plan_issues_no_decision_file_write` |
| H7 | A job's `plan()` raises. | The failure propagates out of `execute()` unchanged and the review is never reached. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_plan_failure_propagates_out_of_execute_unchanged` |
| H8 | A job is executed with no reviewer injected. | `execute()` fails loudly before any converge; no command reaches Nomad. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_missing_reviewer_raises_and_issues_no_converge`; `apt/test_apt_job:TestPlanApplySplit::test_execute_without_a_reviewer_raises_and_issues_no_command` |
| H9 | A run with no terminal has a non-empty plan. | The job reports skipped before any mutating command; nothing is applied and the after-review seam never runs. | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_a_non_interactive_package_review_skips_the_job_instead_of_applying_nothing`; integration:`TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` |
| H10 | A run with no terminal has an empty plan. | The job reports success, nothing converges, and the after-review seam is still skipped (no registry transfer). | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_an_empty_plan_is_still_a_success_and_transfers_nothing`; `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSnippetPush::test_a_run_with_no_terminal_pushes_nothing_even_with_nothing_to_review` |
| H11 | `manual_installs_sync` authors a snippet in its review. | The registry is persisted and pushed to Nomad after the review returns and before any snippet replays. | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSnippetPush::test_push_runs_after_review_and_before_replay_in_execute`; `…::test_snippet_authored_in_review_is_persisted_before_the_push` |
| H12 | Two package jobs are enabled and both machines are diverged. | Each manager's own approved item lands on Nomad's own package manager, so each reviewed before it mutated. | V | integration:`TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| H13 | `apt_sync` writes `/etc/apt`, refreshes and installs in one run. | The user is asked exactly once, before the first of those writes. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestOneReviewPerRun::test_a_run_that_rewrites_etc_apt_still_reviews_exactly_once`; `…::test_a_package_the_target_had_no_candidate_for_is_installed_in_one_review` |
| H14 | A decision record is about to be written (the mark itself), on either machine. | The write passes through the per-command confirmation with a `mutates=` phrase naming the item and the file; declining stops the write; the preceding read prompts for nothing. | U | `tests/unit/test_step_gate.py:TestStateWritesReachTheGate::test_recording_a_decision_is_gated`; `…::test_aborting_leaves_the_file_untouched` |
| H15 | The snippet registry is about to be written, on either machine, or pushed to Nomad. | Both pass through the per-command confirmation with a `mutates=` phrase. | U | `tests/unit/test_step_gate.py:TestStateWritesReachTheGate::test_adding_a_snippet_is_gated`; `…::test_pushing_the_registry_to_the_target_is_gated` |
| H16 | A registry transfer would lose an entry Nomad holds. | Consent is obtained before the transfer; declining aborts the run and sends nothing. | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSnippetRegistryOverwriteGuard::test_lost_target_entry_prompts_and_proceeds_on_confirm`; `…::test_lost_target_entry_aborts_on_decline` |
| H17 | An install whose repository this run writes would take a protected package with it. | The question is put after `/etc/apt` converged but before that install's own command runs. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_keeping_the_package_leaves_the_install_unapplied_and_unfailed`; `…::test_approving_the_removal_installs_and_the_guard_allows_the_collateral_removal` |
| H18 | A machine-gate question exists for the job (Ubuntu Pro). | It is asked before any group is built and before anything is written. | U | `apt/test_apt_esm_gate:TestTheESMAttachmentGate::test_an_unattached_target_is_asked_about_before_anything_is_written` |

### H.2 Only approved work is applied (articles: PKG-FR-ONLY-APPROVED)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H19 | An install-direction item is answered with the act. | Exactly one converge runs for it. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_ticking_only_install_group_yields_zero_removal_commands` |
| H20 | A removal-direction item is answered with the act. | Exactly one converge runs, and it is the removal. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_remove_diff_produces_exactly_one_target_converge_call` |
| H21 | A change-direction item is answered with the act. | Exactly one converge runs for it. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_change_diff_reaches_converge_alongside_install_and_remove` |
| H22 | An item is declined for this run. | Nothing converges for it; the run's other approved items still converge. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_ticking_only_install_group_yields_zero_removal_commands`; `test_package_sync_core:TestDecisionsReachTheLog::test_a_skipped_item_leaves_a_line_where_nothing_else_would` |
| H23 | An item is marked machine-specific. | Nothing converges for it; only the mark is written. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_a_marked_item_converges_nothing_while_its_mark_is_written` |
| H24 | A report-only finding carries an `APPLY` decision (forced). | No converge is ever attempted for it. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_report_only_diff_produces_zero_target_commands` |
| H25 | A dry run has approved items of all four action kinds. | No converge command is issued for any of them. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestConvergeDispatchByAction::test_dry_run_zero_mutating_commands_across_all_four_action_types`; `apt/test_apt_job:TestDryRun::test_dry_run_issues_no_mutating_command` |
| H26 | A repository conflict is answered "overwrite". | Atlas's version of the file is written to Nomad. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestRepositoryConflicts::test_overwriting_a_conflict_writes_the_sources_version` |
| H27 | A repository conflict is declined for this run. | Nothing is written, and every approved package whose origin depended on it fails naming it. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestRepositoryConflicts::test_skipping_a_conflict_writes_nothing_and_fails_the_package_that_needed_it` |
| H28 | A collateral question is answered "apply". | The change that causes it proceeds and the apply-time guard allows the collateral loss. | U | `test_package_review:TestCollateralGroupResolution::test_apply_records_apply`; `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralFlow::test_install_anyway_proceeds_and_guard_allows_the_collateral_removal` |
| H29 | A collateral question is answered "keep the package". | Exactly the changes that cause the loss are left unapplied; no other approved change is cancelled. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralFlow::test_skip_leaves_the_triggering_install_unapproved`; `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralAttribution::test_skip_cancels_only_the_candidate_whose_transaction_causes_it` |
| H30 | An unreproducible item is resolved by writing a snippet. | The snippet is persisted, transferred and replayed in the same run — nothing else is applied for it. | U V | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSameRunApplication::test_on_the_fly_snippet_is_replayed_the_same_run`; `test_end_to_end_sync:TestEndToEndSync::test_full_sync_pipeline_both_directions` |
| H31 | A removal item is declined for this run, and the same item approved on the next. | Nomad keeps the package through the first run and loses it in the second — the decline was a decision, not a deferral. | V | `test_package_sync:TestSkipAlwaysIsInertInBothRoles::test_a_recorded_skip_always_survives_a_forced_apply_in_either_direction` |
| H32 | An already-converged pair is synced. | Zero diffs, zero groups, zero converges, and no command carrying `mutates=`. | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestIdempotency::test_identical_source_and_target_produce_no_diff_no_group_and_no_mutation`; `test_end_to_end_sync:TestEndToEndSync::test_full_sync_pipeline_both_directions` |

### H.3 What batching permits and forbids (articles: PKG-FR-BATCHED, PKG-FR-ASK-AGAIN)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H33 | A group of same-kind items is reviewed. | One screen carries every item with its own decision; there is no second pass over the leftovers. | U | `test_package_review:TestInteractive::test_one_screen_per_group_and_no_second_pass_over_the_leftovers`; `test_review_skip_always:TestThePermanentAnswer::test_no_group_is_ever_asked_about_permanence_a_second_time` |
| H34 | A screen carries several rows of one kind. | Every row is settled in the single pass; the shift-key form sets every row at once. | U | `test_decision_list:TestKeyHandling::test_shift_of_a_key_sets_every_row`; `test_decision_list:TestKeyHandling::test_a_bare_enter_confirms_every_row_at_its_default` |
| H35 | Several groups are reviewed in one round. | The screens come one after another with no executor command between them. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestNoWorkBetweenTheQuestionsOfOneRound::test_no_executor_command_is_issued_between_the_screens_of_one_round` |
| H36 | An `apt_sync` run rewrites `/etc/apt` and installs packages. | The user is asked exactly once for the whole round. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestOneReviewPerRun::test_a_run_that_rewrites_etc_apt_still_reviews_exactly_once` |
| H37 | A question must show a whole file before it can be answered (a pin offered for deletion). | That file is printed and answered on its own screen, not batched behind three other bodies. | U | `test_package_review:TestRemovalGroupContent::test_a_pin_file_is_printed_whole_under_the_machine_that_holds_it` |
| H38 | A question must show two whole versions (a repository conflict). | Each file is answered right after its own two versions are shown. | U | `test_package_review:TestRepoConflictGroupResolution::test_each_conflicting_file_is_answered_right_after_it_is_shown` |
| H39 | A collateral question names a per-item cause. | Each package gets its own screen with its own answer sentences. | U | `test_package_review:TestCollateralGroupResolution::test_each_package_gets_a_decision_screen_of_its_own` |
| H40 | An unreproducible item's act opens an editor. | Each item gets its own screen. | U | `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_each_item_gets_a_decision_screen_of_its_own` |
| H41 | An install's origin is a repository this run has not written yet. | Nothing is asked about its collateral at plan time; no simulation is even attempted. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_the_question_is_absent_from_the_plan_time_review` |
| H42 | The same install, after `/etc/apt` has converged and refreshed. | A second round is asked, carrying the three answers the article requires. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_keeping_the_package_leaves_the_install_unapplied_and_unfailed` (asserts exactly two review rounds, the second carrying the collateral entry) |
| H43 | Two such installs, both unsimulatable at plan time. | The second round asks about both together, in one question, not one per install. | U | apt/collateral:`TestTheLateQuestionIsPutOnceBeforeAnyTransaction::test_two_unsimulatable_installs_share_one_question` |
| H44 | The converge loop reaches its second, third… approved install. | The second round is not re-asked: the question is put once per run. | U | `test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_keeping_the_package_leaves_the_install_unapplied_and_unfailed` |
| H45 | A run in which every install is resolvable on Nomad at plan time. | No second round at all — one review call, no extra rehearsal command. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_the_question_costs_nothing_on_a_run_with_no_late_install` |
| H46 | The second round's answer withdraws an install. | The item is neither applied nor failed; the run names it as not applied by the user's answer. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_the_decision_is_named_in_the_log`; `package_sync_core` `ConvergeItemDeclined` path via `apply()` |
| H47 | The second round is reached with nobody to ask. | Every item is declined for this run — the install is withheld, not pushed through and not failed. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_a_run_with_no_terminal_declines_it` |
| H48 | The permission to ask again is claimed for something the plan already knew. | Not permitted — a consequence already approved at plan time is not put twice. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites` (the `item_id not in approved` filter); `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestOnePackageTwoConsequences::test_approving_the_installs_casualty_does_not_release_the_removals` |
| H175 | A question an article scopes to the work this run APPROVES (a repository deletion, a repository conflict, a collateral package a mark made in this review protects). | It is put in a second round built out of the first round's answers, and that round is a review like any other: its answers govern what applies. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheSecondRoundIsPartOfTheReview::test_the_second_rounds_own_item_is_reviewed_and_converged` |
| H176 | That second round is reached. | Nothing has been changed on Nomad yet: it comes between the first round's answers and the job's first change, so a stopping answer still reaches every change it is about. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheSecondRoundIsPartOfTheReview::test_nothing_is_converged_before_the_second_rounds_answers` |
| H177 | A run with no terminal whose only answerable question is the second round's. | The job is reported skipped, exactly as one whose first round held it — and the second round is never put to nobody. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheSecondRoundIsPartOfTheReview::test_a_question_only_the_second_round_holds_still_skips_a_run_with_nobody_to_ask` |

### H.4 Which questions are asked, and which are never asked separately (articles: PKG-FR-ASK-ABOUT-SOFTWARE, PKG-FR-ASK-WHEN-NOT-DERIVABLE)

Rows H49–H52 assert an absence: the machinery derived from an approved package is never its own question.

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H49 | An approved package comes from a repository Nomad lacks. | The repository appears in no review group in the add or change direction; approving the package is what carries it. | U | `apt/test_apt_probe:TestRepoStateCapture::test_a_repository_never_appears_as_a_review_entry_in_the_add_or_change_direction` |
| H50 | That repository names a signing key. | The key appears in no diff and no review group, in any direction. | U | `apt/test_apt_keyrings:TestKeysAreNotItems::test_no_key_reaches_a_diff_or_a_review_group_in_any_direction` |
| H51 | Atlas holds a pin. | The pin travels with no review line at all. | U | `apt/test_apt_derived:TestPinsStillTravelAsFiles::test_a_pin_file_the_target_lacks_is_written_with_no_review_line`; `apt/test_apt_etc_apt:TestRepoGroupOrdering::test_pins_travel_without_a_review_line_and_land_before_the_sources` |
| H52 | An approved flatpak application needs a remote (and its runtime's remote). | No remote appears in any review group; declining the application is the only way to decline the remote. | U | `test_flatpak_sync:TestRemotesAreDerivedFromApprovedRefs::test_no_remote_appears_in_any_review_group` |
| H53 | Atlas holds an `/etc/apt/apt.conf.d` file Nomad lacks, differs on, or lacks the other way. | It IS asked, in all three directions, with the ordinary decision and the permanent mark. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestAptConfigVocabulary::test_each_direction_names_the_config_file_not_a_package`; `test_block_state_decisions:TestAptRepoItemDecisions::test_declined_config_install_is_recorded_on_source_and_never_re_offered` |
| H54 | Atlas carries ESM repositories and Nomad reports no Ubuntu Pro attachment. | It IS asked, before anything is written and before the job's other questions. | U V | `apt/test_apt_esm_gate:TestTheESMAttachmentGate::test_an_unattached_target_is_asked_about_before_anything_is_written`; integration:`TestTheESMAttachmentGateOnVMs::test_an_unattached_target_skips_apt_sync_and_leaves_etc_apt_untouched` |
| H55 | An approved change would remove, downgrade or upgrade a package installed by hand on Nomad. | It IS asked, per affected package, with three answers. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestPlanTimeCollateral::test_manual_collateral_removal_becomes_a_collateral_review_item` |
| H56 | Overwriting a repository would repoint the origin of software Nomad marked machine-specific. | It IS asked, showing both machines' versions in full. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestRepositoryConflicts::test_a_changed_repository_feeding_a_machine_specific_package_asks_and_shows_both_versions` |
| H57 | Repointing a flatpak remote would move the origin of an application Nomad marked machine-specific. | It IS asked, as a two-answer entry showing both configurations; without the mark the repoint stays silent. | U | `test_flatpak_sync:TestARepointThatMovesAMachineSpecificRefIsAsked::test_a_machine_specific_ref_turns_the_repoint_into_a_two_answer_entry`; `…::test_a_target_only_ref_is_not_machine_specific_and_the_repoint_stays_silent` |
| H58 | Nomad holds a repository Atlas does not, and nothing on Nomad uses it. | It IS asked, naming the repository URLs the file declares. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestRepoRemovalWithheldWhileInUse::test_a_repository_nothing_installs_from_is_offered_with_its_urls` |
| H59 | Nomad holds a pin Atlas does not. | It IS asked, showing the file's content in full. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestTwoAnswerRemovals::test_a_pin_offered_for_deletion_carries_its_whole_content` |
| H60 | A registry transfer would lose or change an entry Nomad holds. | It IS asked, naming the affected entries; declining aborts. | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSnippetRegistryOverwriteGuard::test_lost_target_entry_aborts_on_decline`; `…::test_changed_body_is_non_additive_and_prompts` |
| H61 | Software no manager can reproduce is found on Atlas. | It IS asked — how Nomad should get it — with three answers. | U | `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_the_three_answers_read_as_they_do_on_every_other_screen` |
| H62 | A collateral removal touches only automatically-installed packages. | Nothing is asked; the change is named in the log instead. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestPlanTimeCollateral::test_auto_collateral_removal_produces_no_review_item`; `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestAutoCollateralIsLogged::test_auto_collateral_removal_is_named_in_the_log` |
| H180 | An unowned `/opt` directory on Atlas holds several directories and no file. | It IS asked — which of the two shapes it is — while the run is still planning, because the answer decides what the review lists. | U | `test_manual_installs_sync:TestTheShapeOfAnOptDirectory::test_several_directories_and_no_file_asks_the_user_which_it_is`; `…::test_the_question_is_put_while_the_run_is_planning` |

### H.5 Naming the machines (articles: PKG-FR-NAME-THE-MACHINES)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |

### H.10 The machine-specific mark (articles: PKG-FR-MACHINE-SPECIFIC, PKG-FR-MARK-LIFETIME, PKG-FR-NO-MARK-ON-ORIGIN, PKG-FR-NO-MARK-ON-REPORT, PKG-NG-MARK-ORIGIN, PKG-NG-MARK-PORTABILITY, PKG-FR-MARK-SIDE)

Which machine a conflicting item's mark is about is asked on a screen reached only after the interactive per-group loop, so H249–H255 and H257 are not branches whose VM run has yet to be written: neither route that exists without a person can reach them. The automation variable answers decisions and knows nothing about sides, and a run with no terminal never enters the loop. H256 is the fallback both of those produce, and is the one of the eight a real run can settle; the manual playbook carries the rest.

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H118 | An install-direction item is marked machine-specific. | The mark is written on Atlas, the machine that holds the item; Nomad's file is untouched. | U | `package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_skip_always_on_install_writes_to_source_not_target` |
| H119 | A change-direction item is marked machine-specific and no side answer reaches the write. | The mark is written on Nomad, the copy the batch screen's own permanent answer names. | U | `package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_skip_always_on_change_writes_to_target_not_source` |
| H120 | A removal-direction item is marked machine-specific. | The mark is written on Nomad, the only machine holding it. | U | `package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_skip_always_on_remove_writes_to_target_not_source` |
| H121 | A permanent answer is forced onto an apt hold, in either direction. | Neither machine's decision file is written. | U | `block_state_decisions:TestABlockCanNeverBeRecorded::test_a_forced_permanent_answer_on_an_apt_hold_records_nothing` |
| H122 | The same forced onto a snap refresh hold. | Neither machine's snap decision file is written. | U | `block_state_decisions:TestABlockCanNeverBeRecorded::test_a_forced_permanent_answer_on_a_snap_hold_records_nothing` |
| H123 | The same forced onto a flatpak mask. | Neither machine's flatpak decision file is written. | U | `test_block_state_decisions:TestABlockCanNeverBeRecorded::test_a_forced_permanent_answer_on_a_flatpak_mask_records_nothing` |
| H124 | An `/etc/apt/apt.conf.d` file is marked. | Recorded and never re-offered — apt's own configuration is markable. | U | `test_block_state_decisions:TestAptRepoItemDecisions::test_declined_config_install_is_recorded_on_source_and_never_re_offered` |
| H125 | An item Atlas marked machine-specific is planned again — the machine holding the mark is the one being read | It is proposed in no review, and it is not synced to Nomad — the mark protects it from every action the run takes of its own accord | U V | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_source_held_inert_item_absent_from_the_plans_diffs`; integration:`TestSkipAlwaysIsInertInBothRoles::test_a_recorded_skip_always_survives_a_forced_apply_in_either_direction` |
| H126 | An item Nomad marked machine-specific is planned again — the machine holding the mark is the one being changed | It is proposed in no review, and it is neither removed from Nomad nor overwritten there — the mark protects it from every action the run takes of its own accord | U V | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_target_held_inert_item_absent_even_though_source_also_differs`; integration:`TestSkipAlwaysIsInertInBothRoles::test_a_recorded_skip_always_survives_a_forced_apply_in_either_direction` (reversed direction) |
| H127 | A marked item whose identity exists on no input item (a digest-derived `apt.conf.d` file). | The post-diff pass drops it too, so it never comes back default-checked. | U | `test_block_state_decisions:TestAptRepoItemDecisions::test_declined_config_install_is_recorded_on_source_and_never_re_offered` |
| H128 | A decision file already holds an entry naming a block — a hold, a mask. | It silences nothing: the block still replicates, and so does the software it applies to. | U | `test_block_state_decisions:TestABlockCanNeverBeRecorded::test_a_recorded_snap_hold_decision_does_not_silence_the_replication`, `::test_a_recorded_apt_hold_decision_does_not_silence_the_replication` |
| H129 | An approved change would touch a marked package regardless. | The user is asked, and the question says the package is marked. | U | `test_package_state:TestDecisionScopeReachesCollateral::test_a_mark_protects_a_package_apt_considers_auto_installed`; `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestTheReasonNamesTheGroundThatApplies::test_a_package_only_a_mark_protects_says_so_and_claims_nothing_about_apt` |
| H130 | Marks are recorded and a `folder_sync` run follows. | Every `*.decisions.yaml` is excluded from the transfer, unconditionally, and a `+` rule in the user's own filter cannot re-expose it. | U | `test_folder_sync:TestDecisionFileExcludeFilters::test_home_under_synced_folder_anchors_the_glob_under_user_subdir`; `…::test_user_plus_rule_for_decision_file_does_not_change_command_ordering`; `…::test_unconditional_regardless_of_which_folder_is_synced` |
| H131 | `config_sync` runs. | Only `config.yaml` is sent; no decision file travels. | U | `test_package_state:TestConfigSyncScope::test_copy_config_to_target_sends_only_config_yaml` |

| H134 | A dry run answers an item permanently. | Nothing is recorded. | U | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_no_record_call_when_dry_run` |

| H136 | A repository deletion screen is shown. | The permanent answer is absent from it — a repository cannot be marked. | U | `test_package_review:TestInteractive::test_repo_removal_starts_skipped_and_is_never_offered_permanence`; `test_review_skip_always:TestGroupsNeverOfferedPermanence::test_two_answer_screens_omit_the_permanent_option` |
| H137 | A pin deletion screen is shown. | The permanent answer is absent from it. | U | `apt/test_apt_job:TestTwoAnswerRemovals::test_a_two_answer_group_is_unticked_and_never_offered_permanence` |
| H138 | A repository overwrite (conflict) screen is shown. | The permanent answer is absent from it. | U | `test_review_skip_always:TestGroupsNeverOfferedPermanence::test_two_answer_screens_omit_the_permanent_option`; `test_package_review:TestRepoConflictGroupResolution::test_only_two_answers_are_offered_and_the_row_starts_skipped` |
| H139 | A permanent decision is forced onto every diff of a run holding a repository, a pin and an apt config file. | Only the apt config id reaches a decision file; no `apt:source:` or `apt:pin:` id is ever recorded. | U | `test_block_state_decisions:TestAptRepoItemDecisions::test_no_repository_or_pin_id_can_reach_a_decision_file` |

| H143 | A permanent decision is forced onto a report-only diff. | Nothing is recorded for it. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestBaseHooksAreNoOps::test_base_finalize_hook_writes_nothing` (a `REPORT_ONLY` diff decided `SKIP_ALWAYS` writes no `fake.decisions`) |

| H187 | A run plans while Atlas holds a mark on something Atlas no longer has. | That item is diffed and reviewed in this run, not silenced until the next one. | U | `test_package_state:TestDeadMarksAreDropped::test_a_dead_mark_stops_silencing_its_item_in_the_same_run` |

| H191 | A manager with no way to check what its items are is reconciled. | It drops nothing rather than guessing. | U | `test_package_state:TestDeadMarksAreDropped::test_the_base_job_prunes_nothing` |

### H.11 Abort (articles: PKG-FR-ABORT)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H148 | Ctrl-C at an ordinary decision screen. | The whole sync aborts; the next group's screen is never built. | U | `test_package_review:TestInteractive::test_ctrl_c_at_a_decision_screen_aborts_the_entire_sync`; `test_review_skip_always:TestAbortAndTeardown::test_ctrl_c_at_a_decision_screen_aborts_the_whole_sync` |
| H149 | Ctrl-C at an unreproducible item's screen. | The whole sync aborts, naming the item; the item is not skipped. | U | `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_cancelled_select_aborts_the_entire_sync` |
| H150 | Ctrl-C at a repository-conflict screen. | The whole sync aborts, naming the file. | U | `test_package_review:TestRepoConflictGroupResolution::test_ctrl_c_aborts_the_sync_naming_the_screen` |
| H151 | Ctrl-C at a collateral screen. | The whole sync aborts, naming the package. | U | `test_package_review:TestCollateralGroupResolution::test_ctrl_c_at_a_collateral_screen_aborts_the_whole_sync` |
| H152 | Ctrl-C at a repository/pin deletion screen. | The whole sync aborts, naming the file. | U | `test_package_review:TestRemovalGroupContent::test_ctrl_c_at_a_deletion_screen_aborts_the_whole_sync` |
| H153 | The explicit "stop the sync" answer on a collateral screen. | The whole sync stops, naming the package and the machine; the answer is not read as declining that one item. | U | `test_package_review:TestCollateralGroupResolution::test_abort_raises_sync_aborted_by_user_naming_the_collateral_package`; `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_stopping_ends_the_whole_sync` |
| H154 | Ctrl-C at a machine gate (Ubuntu Pro). | The whole sync aborts and the live display is handed back. | U | `test_package_review:TestAskGate::test_ctrl_c_aborts_the_whole_sync_and_hands_the_display_back` |
| H155 | Abort at the per-command confirmation, by answer or by Ctrl-C/EOF. | The sync aborts; an unanswerable prompt is never read as approval. | U | `tests/unit/test_step_gate.py:TestTerminalUIStepGate::test_abort_raises_and_resumes_ui`; `…::test_unanswerable_prompt_aborts_never_proceeds` |
| H156 | Any review screen raises. | The live display is resumed in a `finally`, so the terminal is always handed back. | U | `package_review:TestInteractive::test_ui_resumed_when_prompt_raises`; `test_package_review:TestUnreproducibleGroupResolution::test_ui_resumed_when_snippet_capture_raises`; `test_package_review:TestTerminalUIReviewer::test_pause_and_resume_both_run_when_the_underlying_prompt_raises` |
| H157 | An abort raised inside a package job reaches the orchestrator. | It is re-raised untouched: no later job runs and the session is recorded aborted, not the job failed. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestOrchestratorPackageItemFailuresContinuation::test_an_abort_raised_in_a_package_job_stops_the_run_untouched` |
| H158 | The letter `a` is proposed as a decision key. | Rejected — `a` is conventionally abort and may never set a decision. | U | `test_decision_list:TestConstruction::test_a_key_may_not_be_the_abort_letter` |
| H159 | The legend is read at any screen. | Abort is not offered beside the answers — it is not listed. | U | `test_decision_list:TestLegend::test_the_legend_does_not_offer_abandoning_the_sync` |

### H.12 Unattended runs and the automation variable (articles: PKG-NG-UNATTENDED, PKG-NG-AUTOMATION-ENV)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H160 | A run with no terminal reaches a package review. | No prompt is constructed at all; every item comes back declined for this run. | U | `test_package_review:TestNonInteractive::test_no_prompt_constructed_and_everything_skipped_once`; `test_review_skip_always:TestGroupsNeverOfferedPermanence::test_non_interactive_run_prompts_nothing` |
| H161 | The same run's items are read afterwards. | Each one is NAMED as not asked and declined for this run, and the groups are printed as the report. | U V | `test_package_review:TestNonInteractive::test_warns_naming_every_item_and_reports_groups`; integration:`TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` |
| H162 | `--yes` is passed on a run with no terminal. | It does not answer the review: the apt job is still reported skipped and nothing is applied. | V | integration:`TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` (the run uses `--yes --allow-first-sync`) |
| H163 | The configuration is searched for a standing-answers key. | There is none — no config key answers a review. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_no_configuration_key_stands_in_for_an_answer` |
| H164 | The automation variable is set to a JSON map of item id to decision. | The review is answered from it, no prompt is constructed and the live display is never paused. | U V | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_automation_env_returns_mapped_decisions_without_prompting`; integration:`TestSkipAlwaysIsInertInBothRoles::test_a_recorded_skip_always_survives_a_forced_apply_in_either_direction` |
| H165 | An item is absent from the map. | It defaults to declined for this run. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_automation_env_returns_mapped_decisions_without_prompting` (only mapped ids get their value; `_decisions_from_automation` fills the rest) |
| H166 | The map names a permanent decision. | It counts as the user's own: the machine-specific mark is written. | V | integration:`TestSkipAlwaysIsInertInBothRoles::test_a_recorded_skip_always_survives_a_forced_apply_in_either_direction` |
| H167 | The map names a permanent decision for an unreproducible item. | The machine-specific mark is written and no snippet is: authoring one takes an editor, which the map has no way to stand in for. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnvCannotResolveAnUnreproducibleItem::test_a_permanent_answer_from_the_map_marks_the_item_and_writes_no_snippet` |
| H168 | The map is malformed JSON. | The run fails loudly before any prompt and before the display is paused. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_malformed_automation_json_fails_loudly_and_prompts_nothing` |
| H169 | The map names a decision that does not exist. | The run fails loudly rather than defaulting to a decline. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_unknown_decision_value_in_automation_json_fails_loudly` |
| H170 | `pc-switcher sync --help` is read. | The variable is not mentioned. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_env_var_not_mentioned_in_cli_help` |
| H171 | The configuration schema is read. | The variable is not a key there. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_no_configuration_key_stands_in_for_an_answer` |
| H172 | The variable is set and a machine gate (Ubuntu Pro) is reached. | It cannot answer the gate: with no terminal the gate answers "nobody was there", and no prompt is built. | U | `test_package_review:TestAskGate::test_the_automation_env_hook_cannot_answer_a_gate` |
| H173 | The variable is set on a run that DOES have a terminal. | It still answers the review silently, unprompted — the accepted cost. | U | `test_package_review:TestAutomationEnv::test_the_variable_answers_a_review_on_a_terminal_too`, `::test_automation_env_returns_mapped_decisions_without_prompting` |

### H.13 Answering a review from the command line (articles: PKG-FR-APPLY-FLAGS, PKG-FR-APPLY-FLAGS-SCOPE, PKG-FR-APPLY-FLAGS-NO-MARK, PKG-FR-APPLY-FLAGS-OUTCOME)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| H218 | Several jobs and both review rounds run under one set of flags. | One policy answers every one of them: the flags are held by the review surface itself, not passed per call. | U | `package_review:TestTerminalUIReviewer::test_the_policy_it_was_built_with_reaches_every_review` |
| H219 | `--apply-package-installs`, and Atlas has a package Nomad lacks. | Applied, and no screen is built for it. | U V | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_install_flag_answers_every_adding_direction`; `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_an_answered_group_is_applied_and_never_put_to_anyone`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (run 1, read off pc2's own dpkg) |
| H220 | The same run also has a removal to decide. | The removal is untouched by that flag: named in a warning and declined for this run, exactly as a run with no terminal leaves it. | U V | `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_an_unanswered_group_is_still_named_and_skipped_for_this_run`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (run 1: still installed on pc2, and named as declined) |
| H221 | `--apply-package-removals`, and Nomad has a package Atlas does not. | Removed; an install in the same review is left to be asked about. | U V | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_removal_flag_answers_every_taking_away_direction`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (run 2) |
| H222 | Both flags together. | Both directions are answered, which is Nomad's package state brought to Atlas's. | U V | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_both_flags_answer_both_directions`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (run 2, over the pair run 1 left) |
| H223 | An item both machines have at different versions, under `--apply-package-installs`. | Answered: converging to Atlas's version is the adding direction, not a loss. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_install_flag_answers_every_adding_direction` |
| H224 | A snap's revision or channel change, under `--apply-package-installs`. | Answered, same as any other change. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_install_flag_answers_every_adding_direction` |
| H225 | A repository or pin deletion, under `--apply-package-removals`. | Answered by the removal flag; the install flag does not reach it. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_removal_flag_answers_every_taking_away_direction` |
| H226 | A protected package an approved change would take with it (`PKG-FR-COLLATERAL-MANUAL`), under `--apply-package-removals`. | Answered by the removal flag: the loss is Nomad's, so it is the removal direction. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_the_removal_flag_answers_every_taking_away_direction` |
| H227 | A repository both machines hold with different content, feeding a package Nomad recorded as its own (`PKG-FR-REPO-CONFLICT`). | No flag answers it: overwriting it moves where software the user told the tool to leave alone comes from. Declined for this run and named. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_no_flag_answers_a_question_the_source_cannot_settle` |
| H228 | An `/etc/apt/apt.conf.d` file Nomad already holds. | No flag answers it: it states how Nomad's own apt behaves. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_no_flag_overwrites_content_the_other_machine_authored` |
| H229 | An item no package manager can install (`PKG-FR-MANUAL-RESOLUTION`). | No flag answers it: the answer is an authored snippet, which takes an editor. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_no_flag_answers_a_question_the_source_cannot_settle` |
| H230 | A reported finding under either flag. | Unaffected — nobody answers a report-only group. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_a_reported_finding_is_answered_by_nobody` |
| H231 | A removal screen, whose rows start at "skip now". | The flag answers as Atlas dictates, not as the screen's own starting position suggests. | U | `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_an_answered_group_is_applied_and_never_put_to_anyone` |
| H232 | Neither flag is passed. | Every group is put to the user exactly as before, and a review nobody answers still skips its job. | U | `package_review:TestWhichGroupsAFlagAnswers::test_no_flag_answers_anything_when_none_was_passed`; `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_a_review_with_no_flag_takes_the_path_it_always_did`; `test_package_sync_core:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_no_policy_on_the_context_leaves_the_job_exactly_as_it_was` |
| H233 | Any group a flag answers. | The answer is never the permanent one — an unattended run may not declare an item specific to a machine. | U | `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_a_flag_never_answers_permanently` |
| H234 | A run with no terminal whose review the flags answered. | It converges what they answered and reports success; skipped is for a review nobody answered. | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_a_run_the_flags_answered_converges_and_is_not_reported_skipped`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (run 1, off the end-of-run outcome block) |
| H235 | A run with no terminal whose review holds only groups no flag answers. | Still reported skipped, as if no flag had been passed. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_a_run_whose_flags_answer_none_of_its_groups_still_skips` |
| H236 | `--yes` is passed with neither apply flag. | It answers no package review: the two are unrelated options. | U | `test_commands:TestApplyPackageFlags::test_yes_alone_answers_no_package_review` |
| H237 | A run WITH a terminal passes one flag, and the review also holds a group that flag does not answer. | The flag's own groups are settled unasked; the user is still asked about the rest. | U | `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_a_terminal_still_gets_asked_about_what_no_flag_answers` |
| H238 | The log of a run the flags answered is read. | One line says the command line answered and names which flags — the per-item decision lines read the same whoever answered. | U | `package_review:TestTheCommandLineAnswersAReview::test_the_log_says_the_command_line_answered_and_names_the_flags` |
| H239 | A flag-answered run finishes. | No decision file is written on either machine. | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_a_flag_answered_run_records_no_machine_specific_mark`; `test_package_sync:TestTheCommandLineAnswersOneDirectionOfTheReview::test_the_install_flag_converges_installs_leaves_removals_and_records_no_mark` (both machines, after both runs) |
| H240 | A flag-answered run reaches the after-review seam. | It runs: an item the flags approved can depend on what that seam transfers. | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_a_run_the_flags_answered_converges_and_is_not_reported_skipped` |
| H241 | `--apply-package-installs` approves a manual install whose snippet is on Atlas. | The registry reaches Nomad before the replay reads it, so the replay is not made from a stale or absent file. | U | `unreproducible_jobs:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_the_registry_is_pushed_before_the_replay_on_a_flag_answered_run` |
| H242 | That same push would lose a snippet entry Nomad holds. | It still aborts: no flag approves a lossy registry transfer. | U | `unreproducible_jobs:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_a_lossy_registry_transfer_still_aborts_under_both_flags` |
| H243 | A flag-answered run reaches an unreproducible item. | No snippet is authored and nothing is stamped: authoring takes an editor, and no flag stands in for one. | U | `unreproducible_jobs:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_no_snippet_is_authored_and_the_registry_is_unchanged` |
| H244 | `--apply-package-installs` alone approves an install whose transaction would lose a protected package. | The install does not converge: the collateral question is unanswered, and a skipped collateral leaves the changes causing it unapproved. Both flags together converge it. | U | `apt/test_apt_collateral:TestTheCommandLineAnswersTheReview::test_the_install_flag_alone_leaves_the_install_unapproved`, `::test_both_flags_converge_the_install_and_its_collateral` |
| H245 | `pc-switcher sync --apply-package-installs --apply-package-removals` is run. | Both flags reach the run. | U | `test_commands:TestApplyPackageFlags::test_both_flags_reach_the_run` |
| H246 | The same run builds a job's context. | The one policy the review surface answers with is the one every package job reads. | U | `test_commands:TestApplyPackageFlags::test_one_policy_reaches_both_the_reviewer_and_every_job_context` |


## J. Outcomes, failure isolation, the dry run, the no-terminal run, the log and what it withholds

### J.1 Success (article: PKG-FR-OUTCOME-SUCCESS)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| J1 | Atlas has one package Nomad lacks; the user approves it and the install succeeds | The job reports success and the package is on Nomad | U V | `test_apt_job:TestContinueOnFailure` (converse); `test_end_to_end_sync:TestEndToEndSync::test_full_sync_pipeline_both_directions` |
| J2 | Atlas and Nomad hold identical item sets, so the job presents nothing | The job reports success; the review is still consulted once, with no groups; no command carrying `mutates=` is issued | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestIdempotency::test_identical_source_and_target_produce_no_diff_no_group_and_no_mutation`; `test_package_sync:TestOneManagerAtATime::test_each_job_alone_converges_its_own_manager_and_the_fifth_run_moves_nothing` |
| J3 | Every item presented is answered "skip this run" | The job reports success; nothing converges | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestDecisionsReachTheLog::test_a_skipped_item_leaves_a_line_where_nothing_else_would` |
| J4 | Every item presented is answered "always skip" | The job reports success; each mark is recorded on its holding machine; nothing converges | U | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_skip_always_on_change_writes_to_target_not_source` |
| J5 | An approved install is withdrawn by a question this run's own first change made answerable (late collateral) | It is counted neither applied nor failed; the job does not fail on its account; the withdrawal and its reason are named | U | `test_apt_collateral:TestCollateralForARepositoryThisRunWrites::test_the_decision_is_named_in_the_log` |
| J6 | The plan holds only report-only findings and a terminal is present | Nothing converges; the job reports success | U | `test_package_review:TestInteractive::test_a_report_only_group_is_printed_and_asks_nothing` |
| J7 | A run whose only items were unreproducible and all answered "skip this run" | Skip-once counts as a resolution; the job reports success | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSkipOnceResolution::test_run_whose_only_items_were_skipped_once_passes` |
| J8 | A job presented nothing and the run has no terminal | Success, not skipped — the target already matches | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_an_empty_plan_is_still_a_success_and_transfers_nothing` |

### J.2 Skipped (article: PKG-FR-OUTCOME-SKIPPED; the two named causes)

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| J9 | The run has no terminal and a job's review holds an item to decide | That job reports skipped, naming why; it does not report success | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_a_non_interactive_package_review_skips_the_job_instead_of_applying_nothing`; `test_package_sync:TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` |
| J10 | Nomad reports no Ubuntu Pro attachment and the user chooses to skip | `apt_sync` reports skipped naming the ESM files and the reason; `/etc/apt` on Nomad is exactly as found | U V | `test_apt_esm_gate:TestTheESMAttachmentGate::test_choosing_skip_raises_job_skipped_and_writes_nothing`; `test_package_sync:TestTheESMAttachmentGateOnVMs::test_an_unattached_target_skips_apt_sync_and_leaves_etc_apt_untouched` |
| J11 | Nomad reports no Ubuntu Pro attachment and there is no terminal to ask at | `apt_sync` reports skipped, saying no TTY was available to choose between attaching and skipping | U | `test_apt_esm_gate:TestTheESMAttachmentGate::test_a_non_interactive_run_skips_the_whole_job` |
| J12 | A skipped job's items included a "always skip" answer that could not be given | No decision file is created or changed on either machine | U V | `test_package_state:TestPipelineWiring::test_no_record_call_when_outcome_was_not_interactive`; `test_package_sync:TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` |
| J13 | A skipped `manual_installs_sync` run | No snippet registry is transferred to Nomad | U | `test_unreproducible_jobs:TestSnippetPush::test_a_run_with_no_terminal_pushes_nothing_even_with_nothing_to_review` |
| J14 | A skipped job | The target is untouched — the skip is raised before any mutating command | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestExecuteSelfContained::test_a_non_interactive_package_review_skips_the_job_instead_of_applying_nothing`; `test_package_sync:TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may` |
| J15 | A job reports skipped, three other jobs follow | The run continues and each following job executes | U | `test_skipped_jobs:TestSkippedJobArm::test_the_orchestrator_records_a_skipped_job_and_runs_the_next_one` |
| J16 | A run in which one job was skipped and no job failed | The session is COMPLETED and the exit code is 0 | U | `test_skipped_jobs:TestSkippedJobArm::test_the_orchestrator_records_a_skipped_job_and_runs_the_next_one`; `test_session_status_from_job_results:TestSessionStatusReflectsJobResults::test_skipped_job_result_is_not_a_failure` |
| J17 | A skipped job's reason | Reaches the run's own report, not only the log | U | `test_skipped_jobs:TestSkippedJobArm::test_the_orchestrator_records_a_skipped_job_and_runs_the_next_one` |
| J18 | Skipped is distinguished from "answered no": a job the user answered entirely with declines | Reports success (J3), while a job nobody could answer reports skipped (J9) | U | J3 + J9 read together |
| J190 | The end-of-run block, over a run in which a package job was skipped | One line for that job carrying the status word and the reason it recorded, and a line for every other job the run configured — including the step that installs pc-switcher on the target, whose own result the block is the only place to read | U V | `test_job_outcome_summary:TestTheBlockNamesEveryJob::test_a_skipped_or_failed_line_carries_the_reason_that_job_recorded`, `::test_each_job_gets_one_line_with_its_status`; `test_package_sync:TestARunWithNobodyToAsk::test_a_non_interactive_run_names_every_item_applies_none_and_mirrors_only_what_it_may`; `test_end_to_end_sync:TestEndToEndSync::test_full_sync_pipeline_both_directions` |

| # | Scenario | Expected | Cov | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| J19 | One approved item's converge command exits non-zero | The job reports failure | U | `test_apt_job:TestContinueOnFailure::test_second_of_three_fails_all_attempted_one_failure_raised` |
| J20 | Three approved items, the middle one fails | All three are attempted; the third still converges | U V | `test_apt_job:TestContinueOnFailure::test_second_of_three_fails_all_attempted_one_failure_raised`; `test_package_sync:TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| J21 | Several items fail in one job | The failures are collected and raised once, after the loop, naming each item | U | `test_snap_sync:TestHoldAndRevisionFailuresArePerItem`; `test_apt_etc_apt:TestRepoGroupTransaction::test_failed_update_restores_changed_deletes_created_records_group_failures`; `test_apt_etc_apt:TestRepoGroupBackupFailure::test_backup_failure_fails_every_group_item_without_crashing` |
| J22 | A converge step refuses an item without attempting the command (a transaction guard) | That item alone fails, naming what it concerns; the loop continues | U | `test_apt_etc_apt:TestRepoGroupOrdering::test_a_failed_derived_repository_write_fails_the_package_that_needed_it`; `test_apt_etc_apt:TestKeyringsDirectoryEnsured::test_directory_preparation_failure_fails_the_item_not_the_run` |
| J23 | Forty items fail in one job | The end-of-run message adds one line for that job, not forty | U | `test_session_status_from_job_results:TestTheOutcomeMessageNamesWhatFailed::test_a_job_with_many_failed_items_stays_on_one_line` |
| J24 | Two jobs fail | The end-of-run message names both jobs and both reasons | U | `test_session_status_from_job_results:TestTheOutcomeMessageNamesWhatFailed::test_each_failed_jobs_reason_reaches_the_message` |
| J25 | A failed job with no recorded reason | The message still names the job | U | `test_session_status_from_job_results:TestTheOutcomeMessageNamesWhatFailed::test_a_failure_without_a_recorded_reason_still_names_its_job` |
| J26 | `apt_sync` fails its items; `snap_sync` follows | `snap_sync` still runs — one failed job does not stop the others | U V | `test_package_sync_core:TestOrchestratorPackageItemFailuresContinuation::test_failing_package_job_does_not_cancel_remaining_jobs`; `test_package_sync:TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| J27 | A package job raises an ordinary exception that is neither a converge failure nor a dead read (a registry transfer error, a parser defect) | That job alone fails; the following jobs run — "any exception out of a package job stays in that job" | U | `test_job_failure_isolation:TestAnyFailureOfAPackageJobStaysInThatJob::test_a_generic_exception_does_not_stop_the_following_job` |
| J28 | That job's failure reason | Reaches its `JobResult` verbatim | U | `test_job_failure_isolation:TestAnyFailureOfAPackageJobStaysInThatJob::test_the_failed_result_carries_what_went_wrong` |
| J29 | A dead read (`ProbeFailed`) out of any job, package or not | Fails only that job; the following job runs | U | `test_job_failure_isolation:TestProbeFailedFailsOnlyItsOwnJob::test_the_orchestrator_records_it_failed_and_runs_the_next_job` |
| J30 | A lock conflict surfaces inside a package job | The whole run ends; no later job runs | U | `test_job_failure_isolation:TestAnyFailureOfAPackageJobStaysInThatJob::test_a_lock_conflict_still_ends_the_run` |
| J31 | A job outside package sync fails | The run still aborts (the #220 boundary this article does not cover) | U | `test_package_sync_core:TestOrchestratorPackageItemFailuresContinuation::test_other_exception_types_still_abort_the_run` |
| J32 | A run that continued past a failed job | The session is FAILED and the CLI exits non-zero | U V | `test_job_failure_isolation:TestProbeFailedFailsOnlyItsOwnJob::test_the_session_is_still_reported_failed`; `test_session_status_from_job_results:TestCliExitCodeFromSessionStatus::test_failed_session_exits_non_zero`; `test_package_sync:TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| J33 | Any failure message a job emits | Names the item, package or file it concerns, and never a role ("the target") | U | `test_machine_naming:TestOutcomeMessages::test_no_outcome_message_names_a_role`; `test_job_failure_isolation:TestProbeFailedFailsOnlyItsOwnJob::test_the_failed_result_carries_the_command_that_did_not_answer` |
| J34 | A converge command's stderr | Is carried into the failure line and the run's summary | U V | `test_package_sync_core` (`_converge_one` path, asserted per manager); `test_package_sync:TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| J191 | The end-of-run block, over a run in which two jobs failed and a third converged its own approved work | All three appear in the one block with their own statuses, and the failures are printed once — not again as a separate list | U V | `test_job_outcome_summary:TestTheBlockNamesEveryJob::test_a_skipped_or_failed_line_carries_the_reason_that_job_recorded`; `test_package_sync:TestAFailureCostsItsOwnItemAndNothingElse::test_the_item_after_a_failure_and_the_jobs_after_its_job_all_still_land` |
| J192 | A failed job's reason quotes a package manager's own output, shaped like Rich markup | It is printed literally: the block renders reasons as text, so `[installed]` neither vanishes nor ends the run after all its work is done | U | `test_job_outcome_summary:TestTheBlockNamesEveryJob::test_a_reason_shaped_like_rich_markup_is_printed_literally` |
